  "short_description": {
    "id": "Colt pixie adalah pixie dalam folklor Inggris selatan yang berwujud kuda poni pucat kusut dan menyesatkan pelancong serta ternak.",
    "en": "The colt pixie is a pixie of southern English folklore that takes the form of a scruffy pale pony to lead travellers and livestock astray.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "colt-pixie-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Colt pixie (juga colepixie, collepixie, colt pixy, cold pixie) adalah makhluk folklor Inggris dari Inggris Selatan dan Barat Daya, terutama New Forest dan Dorset. Menurut mitologi setempat, ia sejenis pixie yang berwujud kuda atau poni pucat dan kusut untuk menyesatkan pelancong dan ternak, mirip will-o'-the-wisp, dan sering dikaitkan dengan Puck.",
      "en": "The colt pixie (also colepixie, collepixie, colt pixy, cold pixie) is a creature of the folklore of Southern and South West England, especially the New Forest and Dorset. Local myth makes it a kind of pixie that turns into a scruffy, pale horse or pony to lead travellers and livestock astray, like a will-o'-the-wisp, and it is often linked with Puck.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "colt-pixie-c01"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Terjemahan Erasmus abad ke-16, Apophthegmatum opus, memuat baris \"memainkan peran Hobgoblin atau Collepixie\". Ungkapan \"sekusut colt pixie\" lazim di New Forest hingga awal abad ke-20, dan dalam dialek Dorset \"to colt-pixy\" berarti menjatuhkan sisa apel setelah panen. Fosil echinoid kadang disebut kepala colt-pixie, dan sebuah gundukan kubur di New Forest dekat Lyndhurst bernama Cold Pixie's Cave.",
      "en": "Erasmus's 16th-century translation Apophthegmatum opus already names the Collepixie beside the Hobgoblin. New Forest folk were still saying 'as ragged as a colt pixie' in the early 20th century, and in Dorset dialect 'to colt-pixy' meant knocking down the apples left after harvest. Fossil echinoids are sometimes called colt-pixies' heads, and a barrow near Lyndhurst in the New Forest is named Cold Pixie's Cave.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "colt-pixie-c02",
        "colt-pixie-c03",
        "colt-pixie-c04"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "source_id": "colt-pixie-s1",
      "quote": "The phrase \"as ragged as a colt pixie\" was common in the New Forest at least as recently as the early 20th century. In the dialect of Dorset \"to colt-pixy\" meant to beat down the remaining apples after a crop has been harvested",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ungkapan as ragged as a colt pixie di New Forest; dialek Dorset to colt-pixy. Hingga awal abad ke-20 (20th century).",
        "en": "Phrase as ragged as a colt pixie in the New Forest; Dorset dialect to colt-pixy. Until the early 20th century."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "colt-pixie-c04",
      "source_id": "colt-pixie-s1",
      "quote": "Fossil echinoids are sometimes called colt-pixies' heads Cold Pixie's Cave is the name of a barrow in the New Forest, near Lyndhurst",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Fosil echinoid disebut kepala colt-pixie; Cold Pixie's Cave dekat Lyndhurst.",
        "en": "Fossil echinoids called colt-pixies' heads; Cold Pixie's Cave near Lyndhurst."
      }
    }
  ],

    "id": "Dando's Dogs atau Devil's Dandy Dogs adalah anjing pemburu mitos dalam folklor Inggris yang ikut dalam Wild Hunt.",
    "en": "Dando's Dogs, or the Devil's Dandy Dogs, are mythical hounds of English folklore that join the Wild Hunt.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dando's Dogs, juga dikenal sebagai Devil's Dandy Dogs, adalah makhluk mitos dalam folklor Inggris: anjing-anjing pemburu yang ikut dalam Wild Hunt. Mereka dikaitkan dengan Black Dogs, istilah umum untuk penampakan anjing hantu.",
      "en": "Dando's Dogs, also called the Devil's Dandy Dogs, are mythical beings of English folklore, hounds that ride with the Wild Hunt. They are linked to Black Dogs, the general term for ghostly canine apparitions.",

        "dando-s-dogs-c01"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Legendanya tentang Dando, pendeta berdosa yang pandai berburu. Setelah berburu pada hari Minggu, ia menghabiskan semua minuman dari rekan-rekannya dan berseru bahwa bila minuman tak dapat ditemukan di bumi, mereka harus pergi ke neraka untuk mendapatkannya. Seorang pemburu asing menawarinya minum, lalu mengambil sebagian buruannya; dalam semua versi, pemburu itu membawa Dando ke neraka sementara anjing-anjingnya mengejar dengan sia-sia. Konon pada pagi buta hari Minggu, Dando's Dogs dapat terdengar memburu atau mencari tuannya.",
      "en": "The legend concerns Dando, a sinful priest and skilled huntsman. After a Sunday hunt he drank all the liquor his companions gave him and declared that if no more could be found on earth they should fetch it from hell.
